Volume - 160 BENGALURU, THURSDAY 13, NOVEMBER, 2025 ( KARTHIKA , 22, SHAKAVARSHA, 1947) Issue 214

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

No.:RC-CFS0ADMN/711/2025 Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1911141 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru,560001
Date: 25-09-2025

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Balihalli Village of Hangal taluk
Haveri District and to constitute a new revenue villages called Bhooteshwar
Nagar as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Balihalli village for conversion of Bhooteshwar Nagar unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/ extension/ part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.
(10124)

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Haveri District.
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared ashabitation are aofcalled Bhooteshwar Nagar village of
Hangal Taluk of Haveri District convert the same to form hamlet/extension with in
the boundary of Balihalli Revenue village and same will herein after be named as
called Bhooteshwar Nagar of Balihalli villagein Hangal Taluk of Haveri District.

Dated: 08-07-2025; And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;

here by makes the following notification, namely:-

of Haveri District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary of

E- 1918719 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, 560001
Date:05-11-2025
PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose s
to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Tarikatte Village of
Chikkanayakanahalli Taluk of Tumakuru District and to constitute a new
revenue villages called Vinayaka Thandya as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of of
Tarikatte village for conversion of Vinayaka Thandya unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or

after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Tumakuru
District.

SCHEDULE-01

Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Vinayaka Thandya village of
Chikkanayakanahalli Taluk of Tumakuru District convert the same to form hamlet
within the boundary of Tarikatte Revenue village and same will herein after be named
as called Vinayaka Thandya of Tarikatte village in Chikkanayakanahalli Taluk
of Tumakuru District.

It is hereby declared that the extent of land indicated in the columns(4) above with

proposed to be converted as habitation area(Gramatana) of Vinayaka Thandya hamlet/part
of Tarikatte village in Chikkanayakanahalli Taluk of Tumakuru District.

The proposal specified above shall come into effect from such date as the
State Government may specify by Notification proposed to be issued under section 5 of
the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act No. 12 of 1964 )

E- 1939193 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, 560001
Date:05-11-2025
PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience
the Government of Karnataka propose s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of D
Rampura Village of Gubbi Taluk of Tumakuru District and to constitute a new revenue
villages called D Rampura Badavane as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified in
Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of D Rampura village for
conversion of D Rampura Badavane unrecorded habitation in to hamlet/part of the said
revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Tumakuru District.

SCHEDULE-01

Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the table
shall be declared as habitation area of D Rampura Badavane village of Gubbi Taluk of
Tumakuru District convert the same to form hamlet within the boundary of D Rampura
Revenue village and same will herein after be named as called
D Rampura Badavane of D Rampura village in Gubbi Taluk of Tumakuru District.

PR-1390
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

No.:RC-CFS/134/2025 Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1938947 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, 560001
Date:04-11-2025

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Uppinahalli Village of Kaduru
Taluk of Chikkamagaluru District and to constitute a new revenue villages called
Uppinahalli Badavane – 1 as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of of
Uppinahalli village for conversion of Uppinahalli Badavane– 1 unrecorded
habitation in to hamlet/part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or

after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Chikkamagaluru District.
SCHEDULE

Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared ashabitation area of Uppinahalli Badavane – 1 village of
Kaduru Taluk of Chikkamagaluru District convert the same to form hamlet within
the boundary of Uppinahalli Revenue village and same will herein after be named as
called Uppinahalli Badavane – 1 of Uppinahalli village in Kaduru Taluk of
Chikkamagaluru District.

It is hereby declared that the extent of land indicated in the columns(4) above
with survey numbers specified in column (3) on which the said unrecorded habitation
located is proposed to be converted as habitation area(Gramatana) of Uppinahalli
Badavane – 1 hamlet/part of Uppinahalli village in Kaduru Taluk of
Chikkamagaluru District.

The proposal specified above shall come into effect from such date as
the State Government may specify by Notification proposed to be issued under
section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 ( Karnataka Act No. 12 of
1964 )

table shall be declared ashabitation area of Ramanayakanahalli village of
KotturuTaluk of Vijayanagara District convert the same to form hamlet within the
boundary of K.Ayyanahalli Revenue village and same will herein after be named as
called Ramanayakanahalli of K.Ayyanahallivillage in KotturuTaluk of
VijayanagaraDistrict.

Date:11-11-2025

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Ravadakunda village of
Sindhanur Taluk Raichur District and to constitute two new revenue villages
called Lakshmi Venkateswara Nagara & Sri rama Nagar as specified in
schedules along with maps with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Raichur
District.

District along with the survey numbers included in coloumn (4) there of

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the
factors specified in clauses ( i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules
1966 such as the population , area of the village, the demand under land revenue in the
village and other head of account and having regard to the administrative convenience
the Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Adi, Village of Nipani Taluka, Belgavi district and to constitute a new villages
called Jainwadi as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers
and new survey numbers assigned to them.

Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state government. Objections or suggestions may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Belgavi District,

SCHEDULE-I
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said Adi
village of Nipani Taluka in Belagavi District to form a new revenue village called as
Jainwadi of Nipani Taluka and Belagavi district along with the survey numbers
included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having

regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alterthe limits of Nulageri Village of Hirekerur
Taluk of Haveri District and to constitute a new revenue villages called
Viganeshwara Nagar as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of of
Nulageri village for conversion of Viganeshwara Nagar unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Haveri District.
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Viganeshwara Nagar village of
Hirekerur Taluk of Haveri District convert the same to form hamlet within the
boundary of Nulageri Revenue village and same will herein after be named as called
Viganeshwara Nagar of Nulageri village in Hirekerur Taluk of Haveri District.

It is hereby declared that the extent of land indicated in the columns(4) above
with survey numbers specified in column (3) on which the said unrecorded habitation
located is proposed to be converted as habitation area(Gramatana) of Viganeshwara
Nagar hamlet/part of Nulageri village in Hirekerur Taluk of Haveri District.

The proposal specified above shall come into effect from such date as
the State Government may specify by Notification proposed to be issued under

section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 ( Karnataka Act No. 12 of

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of T.B.Kavalu Village of Kaduru
Taluk of Chikkamagaluru District and to constitute a new revenue villages called
Niragundi Mata as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of of
T.B.Kavalu village for conversion of Niragundi Mata unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Chikkamagaluru District.

Niragundi Mata of T.B.Kavalu village in Kaduru Taluk of Chikkamagaluru
District.
